Conclusion: MARC框架为临床领域专家提供了一个模型无关、可解释且易于使用的临床推理解决方案，通过多智能体编排方法克服了传统单一LLM提示的局限性，为临床AI应用的部署和调试提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [87] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlayaWorld的改进版本，通过优化条件信号表示和整合方式，使条件信号在潜在表示和时间结构上与生成内容尽可能匹配，显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有AlayaWorld在条件信号的表示和整合方面存在不足，条件信号与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上不够匹配，影响了生成质量和一致性。作者希望改进条件信号的设计，使其更好地引导内容生成。

Method: 提出两大核心改进：（1）用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代基于深度变形的空间记忆；（2）重新设计条件管道，使视觉条件在因果VAE潜在空间编码，并保持与生成视频一致的时间统计特性。具体包括六项修改：运动感知潜在条件、因果编码重渲染空间记忆、像素空间时间记忆窗口对齐、硬记忆dropout、统一VAE编解码协议、移除相机AdaLN分支。

Result: 新版本通过六个具体修改实现了条件信号与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上的紧密匹配，包括运动感知潜在条件、连续因果编码的空间记忆、统一的VAE协议等改进，提升了条件控制的有效性和生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过使条件信号在潜在表示和时间结构上与生成内容高度匹配，改进版AlayaWorld实现了更有效的条件控制和更高质量的生成结果，为条件生成模型的设计提供了新的指导原则。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [88] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuoteBench通过精确最终状态验证，区分了LLM编码代理中命令生成错误与执行传输引入的失败，发现生成能力已接近饱和，边界适应能力才是模型差异的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM编码代理评估仅使用匹配执行分数，无法区分命令生成阶段的错误与生成后执行传输（如序列化、包装、重解析）引入的失败。作者旨在量化这一边界，揭示真实模型能力与部署配置影响之间的差异。

Method: 提出QuoteBench基准，在56个来自14个事故衍生家族的单次任务上，引入一个故意未转义的解析器，跨越生成契约与执行传输边界。通过在插值点转义来重现原始路径结果，从而隔离生成错误与执行传输损害，并测试披露边界信息后的恢复能力。

Result: 在八种同窗口配置中，通过添加解析器重放同一回复导致成功率下降55.4至73.2个百分点；披露边界信息后，六种配置恢复30.4至60.7个百分点，另外两种恢复为零或略负。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距为-3.6点，实际隐藏了-64.3点的损害和+60.7点的补偿。部署配置重新排序了模型：26个可比较对中有一个明确的反转，另有四个位于单任务边界上。

Conclusion: 原始生成能力已接近前沿饱和，边界适应能力仍是区分模型的关键。命令发布代理的评估应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器，而非将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [89] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OmniScientist，一个端到端、全模态的AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据进行多学科研究。在36个跨5个学科家族的真实数据案例中，系统完成了从原始数据到编译稿件的完整流程，平均论文得分6.3。直接感知相比仅使用预计算特征的方法在所有7个评估维度上均有提升，赢得了85%的对比评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统主要基于文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要进行推理，无法获取科学发现所依赖的关键空间、时间、跨通道和程序关系等证据。仅覆盖工作流程并不能提供科学发现所需的完整证据访问能力。

Method: 提出OmniScientist系统，包含感知层和3个自主智能体（构思、实验、写作），在确定性流程中运行。通过代码运行想法、严谨性和声明检查，实现新颖性筛选、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。系统能够处理图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图表等多种模态。

Result: 在36个跨5个学科家族、4类科学证据的真实数据案例上评估，系统在所有案例中完成了从原始数据到编译稿件的完整路径。使用参考推理骨干获得平均论文得分6.3。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体进行配对比较，直接感知在所有7个评估维度上均有改善，赢得了85%的直接对比评判。

Conclusion: 生命周期级的感知能力对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要，为实现广泛能力的AI科学家提供了实用路径。直接感知相比传统预计算特征方法具有显著优势，能够更全面地支持科学研究过程。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [DualPI2 Active Queue Management in ns-3: Implementation And Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12513)
*Maria Eduarda Veras,Eduardo Freitas,Assis T. de Oliveira Filho,Djamel Sadok,Judith Kelner*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在ns-3网络模拟器中实现了高保真的DualPI2 AQM模型，并在25种不同BDP场景下验证了其与Linux内核实现的一致性，为L4S研究提供了可靠的仿真工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超低延迟应用需求的增长，L4S标准得到广泛采用，但ns-3模拟器缺乏高保真且最新的DualPI2 AQM模型，无法准确模拟L4S核心组件的行为，限制了研究人员对L4S性能的评估和推进。

Method: 在ns-3中实现了完整的DualPI2 AQM模型，严格遵循官方Linux内核架构，引入了先前模拟中缺失的关键机制，包括基于信用的加权轮询(WRR)调度、步进标记和过载保护机制，并在25种不同带宽延迟积(BDP)场景下与物理Linux测试平台进行了对比验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，该ns-3模型能够准确复制真实世界的行为，在多种网络拓扑中确保严格的吞吐量公平性和队列延迟隔离，验证了仿真模型的高保真性和准确性。

Conclusion: 该经过验证的DualPI2模型为研究社区提供了一个可靠的仿真工具，使研究人员能够在从数据中心到无线家庭和办公环境等多样化网络拓扑中评估和推进L4S性能，填补了ns-3模拟器在L4S研究方面的空白。

Abstract: The demand for ultra-low latency applications necessitates advanced network architectures like the Low Latency, Low Loss, and Scalable Throughput (L4S) standard. A core component of L4S is the DualPI2 Active Queue Management (AQM), which ensures the safe coexistence of scalable and classic traffic. Despite L4S's growing adoption, the ns-3 network simulator lacks a high-fidelity, up-to-date DualPI2 model. This paper presents a comprehensive implementation of the DualPI2 AQM in ns-3, while also mirroring the official Linux Kernel architecture. Our model incorporates representative mechanisms previously absent in simulation, such as credit-based Weighted Round Robin (WRR) scheduling, step-marking, and overload protection. To guarantee simulation accuracy, we validate our implementation against a physical Linux testbed across 25 diverse Bandwidth-Delay Product (BDP) scenarios. Results demonstrate that our ns-3 model replicates real-world behavior, ensuring strict throughput fairness and queue delay isolation. Ultimately, this validated model equips the research community with a robust tool to evaluate and advance L4S performance across diverse network topologies, ranging from data centers to wireless home and office environments.

</details>


### [91] [Digital Twin Satellite Networks: A Paradigm for Intelligent, Efficient, and Resilient Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12865)
*Mustafa Alhassan,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了数字孪生卫星网络(DTSN)框架，通过实时遥测、预测智能和弹性控制实现LEO卫星星座的可靠智能管理，仿真验证了其在威胁环境下能有效隔离故障节点并保持服务连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星巨型星座成为下一代非地面网络的重要组成部分，但其运营面临网络拓扑快速变化、星间链路间歇性、硬件干扰和严格的SWaP限制等挑战。现有基于数字孪生、SDN和O-RAN的方法虽然提供了有用的构建模块，但无法完全支持实时、预测性和平台感知的网络运营。

Method: 提出数字孪生卫星网络(DTSN)框架，构建连接物理卫星网络与同步虚拟孪生的闭环架构，融合实时遥测、感知通信一体化(ISAC)、预测智能和弹性导向控制。通过NASA 42航天器仿真器和Python DT桥接器开发星座级跨域协同仿真平台进行验证。

Result: 在600秒飞行窗口内，DT持续接收物理遥测数据，管理包括运动漂移、硬件故障和对抗性干扰的多域威胁环境。利用预测前瞻机制和指数传感器恢复模型，框架成功隔离受影响节点并触发主动网络重构，确保了服务连续性和动态网络弹性。

Conclusion: DTSN框架展现出支持预测性和弹性导向卫星网络运营的潜力，为LEO卫星星座的可靠智能管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Satellite mega-constellations in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) are becoming an important part of next-generation non-terrestrial networks, but their operation remains challenging because of fast network topology variation, intermittent inter-satellite links, hardware disturbances, and strict Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints. Existing approaches based on Digital Twin (DT), Digital Twin Network (DTN), Software-Defined Networking (SDN), and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) provide useful building blocks for intelligent satellite networking, but they do not fully support real-time, predictive, and platform-aware network operation. In this paper, we propose a Digital Twin Satellite Network (DTSN) framework as a closed-loop architecture for reliable and intelligent management of LEO satellite constellations. The proposed framework connects the physical satellite network with a synchronized virtual twin and combines real-time telemetry, Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC), predictive intelligence, and resilience-oriented control. To validate the concept, we develop a constellation-scale cross-domain co-simulation using the NASA 42 spacecraft simulator and a Python-based DT bridge for a LEO constellation. The DT continuously ingests physical telemetry to manage a multi-domain threat environment, encompassing kinematic drift, hardware failures, and adversarial jamming over a 600-second flight window. By leveraging a predictive lookahead mechanism and an exponential sensor recovery model, the framework successfully isolates compromised nodes and triggers proactive network reconfiguration, thereby ensuring uninterrupted service and dynamic network resilience. These results show the potential of DTSN to support predictive and resilience-oriented satellite network operations.

</details>


### [92] [Pareto-Aware Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Online Resource Allocation in RIS-assisted Large-Scale IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13032)
*Wenhan Xu,Jiashuo Jiang,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PAAERL的降维分层强化学习框架，通过用低维权重向量表示Pareto最优前沿并结合自编码器压缩，解决了RIS辅助IoT网络中高维变量优化和实时计算挑战，显著降低了训练时间和网络成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在5G/6G网络中，可重构智能表面（RIS）技术虽然关键，但在优化RIS辅助的多用户系统时面临两大核心挑战：物理层变量维度极高，且速率域存在非凸Pareto最优集，这导致实时应用中的计算复杂度过高，难以满足实时性需求。

Method: 提出Pareto感知自编码器辅助强化学习（PAAERL）框架。方法分两个阶段：首先，利用严格表示Pareto最优前沿的低维权重向量替代高维连续RIS波束成形变量，在理论上避免几何信息丢失；其次，集成自编码器架构进行数据驱动的二次压缩，将优先级空间映射到高度压缩的连续潜在动作空间，以缓解密集网络中的维度灾难问题。

Result: 在包括多用户移动边缘计算（MEC）网络在内的多种实际通信场景仿真中，PAAERL框架大幅缩短了离线训练时间，加速了在线策略收敛，并相比现有基准方法显著降低了整体网络成本，验证了其在下一代智能IoT环境中的扩展性和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的PAAERL框架有效解决了RIS辅助IoT网络中资源分配优化的维度和计算难题，证明了其在降低计算开销、提升实时性能方面的优越性，为下一代智能IoT网络的高效资源管理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of 5G and emerging 6G networks, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) have become a critical technology for enhancing wireless communication scenarios. However, optimizing RIS-assisted multi-user systems typically introduces high-dimensional physical layer variables and non-convex Pareto-optimal rate sets, posing severe computational challenges for real-time applications. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a dimension-reduced, hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework, termed Pareto-aware autoencoder-assisted RL (PAAERL), to optimize online resource allocation in RIS-assisted Internet of Things (IoT) networks. Our approach first substitutes high-dimensional continuous RIS beamforming variables with lower-dimensional weight vectors that strictly represent the Pareto-optimal frontier, theoretically avoiding geometric information loss across both convex and non-convex rate regions. To further mitigate the curse of dimensionality in dense networks, an autoencoder architecture is integrated to execute a secondary, data-driven compression phase, mapping the priority space into a highly condensed continuous latent action space. Extensive simulations conducted across practical communication scenarios, including multi-user mobile edge computing (MEC) networks, demonstrate that the proposed PAAERL framework drastically reduces offline training times, accelerates online policy convergence, and significantly decreases overall network costs compared to state-of-the-art benchmarks, underscoring its exceptional scalability and practical viability for next-generation intelligent IoT environments.

</details>

### [94] [SCALE-Sim EVA: Design Principles for an Extensible, Visualizable, and Adaptable Accelerator Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12354)
*Jingtian Dang,Ritik Raj,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了SCALE-Sim EVA，一个可扩展、可可视化、可适应的AI加速器模拟框架，支持编译器中间表示（IR）感知的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI加速器日益集成异构计算单元、层次化存储器、本地缓冲区和专用数据传输路径。这种硬件多样性使得固定的加速器模拟器难以扩展到其原始执行模型之外，缺乏灵活性。

Method: EVA将工作负载表示为基于张量的命令，跟踪运行时张量放置和就绪状态，并在可组合的硬件组件上执行命令。它通过张量就绪性、硬件单元可用性和建模的操作/传输延迟计算周期时序，而非回放地址级周期轨迹。其命令分解机制桥接了编译器级IR粒度和硬件级执行粒度，支持全局张量操作在硬件层次结构中局部降低和调度。

Result: EVA能够生成开放的命令和存储轨迹，用于执行时间线、内存占用和资源争用的可视化分析。实现了对异构加速器的灵活建模，支持用户定义的功能单元和内存行为。

Conclusion: SCALE-Sim EVA为异构AI加速器提供了一个灵活、可扩展的模拟框架，通过IR感知的设计有效连接了编译器抽象和硬件执行细节。

Abstract: Modern AI accelerators increasingly combine heterogeneous compute units, hierarchical memories, local buffers, and specialized data movement paths. This diversity makes fixed accelerator simulators difficult to extend beyond their original execution model. We present SCALE-Sim EVA, an extensible, visualizable, and adaptable simulation framework for IR-aware accelerator modeling. EVA represents workloads as tensor-based commands, tracks runtime tensor placement and readiness, and executes commands on composable hardware components with user-defined functional units and memory behavior. Instead of replaying address-level cycle traces, EVA computes cycle timing from tensor readiness, hardware-unit availability, and modeled operation or transfer latency. Its command decomposition mechanism bridges compiler-level IR granularity and hardware-level execution granularity, allowing global tensor operations to be lowered and scheduled locally across a hardware hierarchy. EVA also emits open command and storage traces for visual analysis of execution timelines, memory occupancy, and resource contention.

</details>


### [95] [Lonic: Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design for Energy-Efficient Fully Local Online SNN Training with INT4 Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12500)
*Peilin Chen,Xiaoxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lonic，一种针对脉冲神经网络的算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过INT4低精度训练算法和专用硬件优化，相比GPU和现有ASIC加速器实现了显著的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脉冲神经网络在线训练算法虽然在理论上具有时空局部性优势，但未能有效转化为实际硬件设备上的能效优势。存在算法优势与硬件实现效率之间的鸿沟，缺乏针对完全局部在线监督学习的专用硬件加速方案。

Method: 采用算法-硬件协同设计方法。算法层面实现INT4低精度训练算法以保持精度的同时降低计算复杂度；硬件层面设计可重构无乘法器整数处理单元阵列、双优化零门控策略、时序前缀加速的局部学习数据流以及低精度权重移动机制，全面提升训练效率。

Result: 相比Apple M4和Nvidia V100 GPU，Lonic分别实现17.44倍和66.28倍的平均能效提升，以及3.25倍和1.02倍的加速比。相比类TPU的ASIC和H2Learn加速器，分别实现15.95倍（14.64倍面积效率）和1.52倍（7.28倍面积效率）的能效提升。

Conclusion: Lonic成功地将完全局部在线SNN学习的算法优势转化为实际硬件效率，证明了算法-硬件协同设计在能效优化方面的有效性，为SNN的低功耗应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have recently attracted increasing attention as an energy-efficient learning paradigm. Existing works also propose temporally and fully local online SNN training algorithms to address memory and computation overhead. However, they do not consider whether the algorithmic advantages can be effectively translated into real-device efficiency. To address this challenge, we present Lonic, an algorithm-hardware co-design for energy-efficient and scalable fully local online supervised SNN learning. On the algorithm side, we implement an INT4 low-precision training algorithm for fully local online SNN learning while maintaining accuracy. On the hardware side, to leverage the benefits of the proposed algorithm, we introduce reconfigurable multiplier-free integer PE arrays, dual-optimization zero-gating strategy, temporal prefix-accelerated local learning dataflow, and low-precision weight movement to significantly improve training efficiency. Compared to Apple M4 and Nvidia V100 GPUs, Lonic achieves average energy efficiency improvements of 17.44x and 66.28x, respectively, along with speedups of 3.25x and 1.02x, respectively. Moreover, Lonic achieves 15.95x (14.64x) and 1.52x (7.28x) energy efficiency (area efficiency) over ASIC TPU-like and H2Learn accelerators, respectively. The code for Lonic is available at https://github.com/peilin-chen/Lonic.

</details>


### [96] [GateTruth: Auditing the Rigor of RTL Design Benchmarks via Mutation Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12635)
*Meet Bhadra*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GateTruth，一个用于审计RTL基准测试测试平台严格性的突变测试引擎和方法论，发现现有基准测试存在测试平台质量不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTL硬件设计的大型语言模型基准测试快速增加，但都没有应用突变测试来量化测试平台质量。一个从不失败的测试平台可能只是没有刺激到实际损坏的逻辑，而不是证明设计正确。

Method: 引入GateTruth突变测试引擎，向参考设计注入确定性、有种子的语义突变体集合，测量测试平台捕获的突变体比例。使用68任务的双轨参考套件进行验证，包括60个规范到RTL生成任务和8个代理修复任务。

Result: 在自建的68任务参考套件中，46个测试平台杀死至少95%的注入突变体；对RTLLM v2.0的审计显示，72%的设计低于95%标准，三个得分为0%。发现4096-token输出限制会截断部分模型，调整至16384 tokens后模型排名发生显著变化。

Conclusion: 突变杀灭认证应成为RTL生成基准测试的标准报告要求。NVIDIA的CVDP基准测试因缺少参考解决方案而无法进行突变测试审计。

Abstract: Benchmarks for evaluating large language models on register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware design have proliferated rapidly, yet none reports having applied mutation testing, an established hardware-verification technique for quantifying testbench quality, to ask whether its own testbenches are trustworthy. A testbench that never fails is not evidence of a correct design; it may simply never stimulate the logic that is actually broken. We introduce GateTruth, a mutation-testing engine and methodology for auditing RTL benchmark testbench rigor: inject a deterministic, seeded set of semantic mutants into a reference design and measure what fraction the testbench catches.
  We validate the methodology against our own 68-task, dual-track reference suite -- 60 specification-to-RTL generation tasks and 8 agentic-repair tasks, scored through a pinned, deterministic synthesis-to-timing flow with correctness enforced as a strict gate -- certifying that 46 of 60 Track A testbenches kill at least 95% of injected mutants under sequential, reproducible execution; we disclose why the other 14 do not, including a Goodhart effect on testbenches revised to pass this gate. We then point the same engine, unmodified, at RTLLM v2.0, a widely adopted external benchmark: of 46 auditable designs, 72% fall below the 95% floor our own suite is held to, and three score 0% outright.
  A comparable audit of NVIDIA's CVDP benchmark is structurally impossible: its public release withholds reference solutions, removing the golden RTL mutation testing requires. Auditing our own instrument also surfaced a second finding: an initially uniform 4096-token output cap silently truncated three of seven evaluated models, and re-running at 16,384 tokens moved one model from fifth place to first. We argue mutation-kill certification should become a standard reporting requirement for RTL-generation benchmarks generally.

</details>


### [97] [Spec-Driven Hardware Evolution via Executable Contract Refinement and Proof-Guided RTL Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12684)
*Shibo Zhao,Yang Zhang,Mengxia Tao,Baoqi Zhang,Kezhi Li,Qiang Xu,Binwu Zhu,Hao Yan,Min Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于规范驱动的硬件演化框架，通过将功能需求转化为可执行契约，实现了对可信遗留RTL设计的语义版本迭代，而非从零开始生成RTL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的硬件研究主要关注从提示词到RTL的生成，忽视了对已有可信设计进行语义版本演化的支持。硬件开发本质上是演进的，主要版本通常通过修改预期行为并更新已验证的实现来完成，而不是从头重新生成RTL。

Method: 提出规范驱动的硬件演化方法，将功能需求精化为可执行契约。框架分为四个阶段：Specify（契约制定）、Plan（推导跨版本语义差异并定位受影响的RTL区域）、Implement（遗留感知的RTL更新）、Validate（证明引导的检查与迭代修复）。其中Plan阶段借助基于变异的语义探测技术，Implement和Validate阶段在证明引导下进行自动检查与修复。

Result: 在TPU数据通路模块的数据格式变更场景下进行受控版本演化案例研究，验证了契约驱动硬件演化的可行性。实验结果表明，后端工作流能够有效地将已验证的遗留RTL在已审查的可执行契约引导下推进到下一版本的功能收敛。

Conclusion: 基于契约的硬件演化框架为遗留RTL设计的版本迭代提供了一种可行且有效的解决方案，能够自动完成从契约批准到RTL更新与验证的完整流程，为硬件设计演进提供了新的方法论支撑。

Abstract: Hardware development is inherently evolutionary: major revisions typically begin by changing intended behavior and then updating a previously validated implementation, rather than regenerating RTL from scratch. Yet most recent LLM-based hardware research still frames the task primarily as prompt-to-RTL generation, offering limited support for semantic version evolution of trusted legacy designs. We present spec-driven hardware evolution, a contract-centered formulation for RTL version iteration. Instead of treating a new feature request as a direct prompt for RTL generation, we refine it into a reviewed executable contract for the next version. This contract specifies what must hold at the externally visible transactional level through a behavior-level reference together with explicit observation and checking semantics, while leaving how the change is realized in RTL to the evolution process. Based on this formulation, we organize hardware evolution into four stages: Specify, Plan, Implement, and Validate. After contract approval, the remaining stages proceed automatically: Plan derives cross-version semantic deltas and localizes affected RTL regions, aided by mutation-based semantic probing; Implement and Validate then perform legacy-aware RTL update under proof-guided checking and iterative repair. We evaluate the framework on a controlled version-evolution case study of a representative TPU datapath block under data-format changes. The results support the feasibility of contract-driven hardware evolution and demonstrate that the proposed backend workflow can effectively drive validated legacy RTL toward next-version functional convergence under a reviewed executable contract. An anonymous artifact for reproducibility is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SDHE-3A6C.

</details>


### [98] [SynAct: A Reasoning-Acting Large Language Model Agent for Adaptive Synthesis Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12751)
*Fangzhou Liu,Peiyi Han,Jiawei Liu,Yuan Pu,Zhuolun He,Rongliang Fu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出SynAct，一种自适应闭环LLM智能体，通过迭代分析综合报告并针对性地发出优化指令，将平均最差负松弛（WNS）降低至引导综合的27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑综合将RTL设计转换为门级网表，PPA结果对优化指令的选择高度敏感，导致综合调优既是高维问题又计算昂贵。现有方法分为两类：自动化方法在固定动作空间上进行黑盒搜索，决策级可解释性有限；LLM方法预先生成静态脚本，无法适应电路状态的动态变化。

Method: 提出SynAct，一种自适应闭环LLM推理-执行智能体。它迭代式地诊断实时综合报告，推理当前电路状态、检索到的工具知识和历史优化经验，从而发出针对性的优化指令。系统专注于改善时序（特别是WNS），同时保持面积和功耗的平衡。

Result: 在商业综合工具上对14个设计进行实验，SynAct将平均WNS降低至引导综合的27%，显著改善了时序性能。

Conclusion: SynAct通过闭环自适应推理机制，有效解决了逻辑综合调优中的高维性和动态适应性问题，在保持PPA平衡的同时显著提升了时序性能。

Abstract: Logic synthesis transforms RTL designs into gate-level netlists, where PPA results are highly sensitive to the choice of optimization commands, making synthesis tuning both high-dimensional and expensive. Previous approaches fall into two categories: automated methods, which perform black-box search over fixed action spaces with limited decision-level interpretability, and LLM-based methods, which typically generate static scripts upfront and cannot adapt to evolving circuit states. We present SynAct, an adaptive closed-loop LLM reasoning--acting agent that iteratively diagnoses live synthesis reports and reasons over the current circuit state, retrieved tool knowledge, and historical optimization experience to issue targeted commands. SynAct focuses on improving timing, particularly worst negative slack (WNS), while maintaining balanced area and power trade-offs. Experiments on a commercial synthesis tool across 14 designs show that SynAct reduces average WNS to 27% of that from bootstrap synthesis.

</details>


### [99] [Dryas: A Reprogrammable Engine for High-Speed Interconnect Tracing and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12934)
*Manuel Bröchin,Tom Kuchler,Michael Giardino,David Cock,Timothy Roscoe*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dryas，一个开源的FPGA互连分析工具，利用基于NFA的可重配置过滤引擎，在不重新编程FPGA的情况下，实现对高速互连的实时追踪和调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统中异构组件的普及伴随着更高带宽和更低延迟的互连，但这些接口和协议极其复杂，开发、调试和分析FPGA实现需要大量工程工作。一旦功能实现完成，优化控制器和相关软件需要处理可能数百GB的追踪数据，现有方法效率低下。

Method: 开发Dryas工具，使用最少硬件资源实现运行时可重编程的overlay引擎。该过滤引擎基于非确定性有限自动机(NFA)，通过状态转换元素(STE)高效实现，可在缓存行粒度追踪事件，并能在不到一秒内更改过滤器而无需重新编程FPGA或干扰运行中的应用程序。

Result: 成功在一个非常高速(30 GiB/s)、低延迟(200 ns)互连的FPGA实现上部署Dryas。实验证明了工具在调试FPGA互连实现和分析缓存行为两个用例中的有效性，能够在全速运行时检测罕见、复杂或瞬态事件。

Conclusion: Dryas提供了一种高效的互连分析解决方案，不仅能够调试互连本身的实现，还能分析加速应用的行为，为异构计算系统的开发和优化提供了重要工具支持。

Abstract: The proliferation of heterogeneous components in modern computing systems has been accompanied by new higher bandwidth and lower latency interconnects. These interfaces and protocols are enormously complex and the process of developing, debugging, and analyzing FPGA-based implementations requires significant engineering work. Moreover, once a functional implementation is completed, optimization of the controller and associated software requires processing potentially hundreds of gigabytes of trace data.
  In this paper, we present Dryas, an open source tool for analyzing such an interconnect. We developed our tool, using minimal hardware resources, alongside an FPGA implementation of a very high speed, low latency (30~GiB/s, 200~ns) interconnect. With our run-time reprogrammable overlay engine we can inspect this interconnect to find rare, complex, or transient events even at full operation. This filtering engine is based on non-deterministic finite automata (NFAs), efficiently implemented using state transition elements (STEs), allowing us to trace events at a cache-line granularity. Moreover we can change the filters in less than a second, without reprogramming the FPGA or interfering with the running application. This data enables not only debugging the implementation of the interconnect itself, but analyzing the behavior of accelerated applications.
  We examine the mathematical basis for using NFAs and describe their implementation on a real coherent CPU-FPGA research platform. We then evaluate the scalability of Dryas for various size NFAs, followed by two different use cases: debugging FPGA implementation of the interconnect and analyzing cache behavior.

</details>


### [100] [Why Do Prefetchers Fail? Let Agents Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13027)
*Xiangfeng Sun,Ceyu Xu,Ningzhi Ai,Zeyu Zhu,Yiyang Yuan,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次实现基于智能体自动探索的硬件预取器设计流程MoP，通过迭代定位性能异常、诊断根因并合成专用子预取器，最终在SPEC基准上实现61.1%的IPC加速，超越多个人工设计的SOTA方案14.5%–23.6%，并完成6nm RTL综合验证其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硬件预取器设计依赖专家人工分析大规模执行与访存痕迹，过程繁琐、试错成本高，且无法系统性地检查跨多样负载的十亿级指令轨迹，缺乏提升的保证，亟需一种自动化、性能异常驱动的设计方法来突破这些局限。

Method: 提出性能异常驱动的自动研究流程，迭代地：定位高影响的未命中事件至程序计数器，向智能体提供硬件日志、源码与切片轨迹，通过最小可复现案例验证诊断，并为反复出现的模式族合成专用子预取器。利用仿真器在环反馈不断修正，最终构建Mixture of Prefetchers（MoP）。

Result: 在SPEC CPU2006与SPEC CPU2017上，MoP相比无预取实现61.1%的几何平均IPC加速，较人工设计的Alecto、Berti、Pythia分别提升14.5%、21.6%、23.6%；6nm库RTL综合显示片上存储110 KB、面积0.0347 mm²，验证其实用性与面积/存储代价可控。

Conclusion: 首次实证表明，智能体驱动的硬件设计流程能够产出RTL可行、超越现有人工SOTA预取器的方案，在未见负载上取得显著性能增益，标志着自动化硬件设计从理论到实践的重要跨越。

Abstract: Hardware prefetchers are crucial to processor performance, yet their design remains labor-intensive and expert-driven. Architects inspect execution and memory-access traces, identify patterns, translate them into online hardware heuristics, and evaluate them in simulation, often with no guarantee of improvement. Human experts cannot systematically inspect billion-instruction traces across diverse real-world workloads.
  We present a performance-anomaly-driven autoresearch flow that repeatedly asks why a deployed prefetcher fails and uses the diagnoses to construct the Mixture of Prefetchers (MoP). Each iteration localizes high-impact unexplained misses to program counters, gives agents hardware logs, source code, and sliced traces, validates diagnoses through runnable minimal cases, and synthesizes specialized sub-prefetchers for recurring pattern families. Measured performance and remaining anomalies feed subsequent iterations, enabling simulator-in-the-loop discovery beyond model priors.
  The campaign consumes 1.91 billion DeepSeek V4 Pro tokens. On SPEC CPU2006 and SPEC CPU2017, MoP achieves a 61.1% geomean IPC speedup over no prefetching, outperforming the human-designed Alecto, Berti, and Pythia prefetchers by 14.5%, 21.6%, and 23.6%, respectively. RTL synthesis in a 6nm library reports 110 KB of on-chip storage and 0.0347 mm^2 area. To our knowledge, this is the first empirical demonstration that an agent-driven hardware-design process can produce an RTL-practical prefetcher that outperforms state-of-the-art human designs on unseen workloads.

</details>


### [101] [Potential Applications of HBF in LLM Serving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13127)
*Yihan Yin,Yinlun Zhao,Zhixin Yun,Guanying Wu,Feng Zhu,Kai Tao,Shu Li,Fei Huang,Zhe Zhang,Shuangchen Li,Hongzhong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出将高带宽闪存（HBF）作为GPU内存层次结构的容量扩展，以缓解LLM服务中的内存容量瓶颈，通过保留模型权重来提升MoE和多模型服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM模型权重、KV缓存及服务模型变体数量的持续增长，现有基于HBM的服务系统面临日益严重的内存容量约束，亟需容量导向的内存扩展方案。

Method: 研究HBF集成到GPU内存层次结构的方法，确保不损害计算芯片所需的带宽；将新增容量的系统级价值建模为读多型模型状态对象的驻留集扩展，在保留HBM执行路径的前提下利用HBF扩展模型权重驻留集。

Result: 仿真结果表明：在保留HBM驻留执行路径的同时使用HBF扩展模型权重驻留集，HBF可通过增加专家副本数量改善MoE服务、通过减少模型加载并支持热模型复制来改善多模型服务。

Conclusion: HBF作为容量导向的HBM扩展具有系统级价值，其效益实现的关键在于保持HBM驻留执行路径并使用HBF扩展模型权重驻留集，从而有效缓解LLM服务的内存容量约束。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly constrained by memory capacity as model weights, KV caches, and the number of served model variants continue to grow. This report examines High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) as a capacity-oriented extension to HBM-based serving systems. We first discuss how HBF can be integrated into the GPU memory hierarchy without undermining the bandwidth expected by the compute die. We then model the system-level value of added capacity as expanded residency for read-mostly model-state objects. Under this view, HBF can improve MoE serving by enabling more expert replicas and can improve multi-model serving by reducing model loading and supporting hot-model replication. Our simulation results show that these benefits depend on preserving the HBM-resident execution path while using HBF to expand the resident set of model weights.

</details>


### [102] [ROLoad-PMP: Securing Sensitive Operations for Kernels and Bare-Metal Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13287)
*Wende Tan,Chenyang Li,Yangyu Chen,Yuan Li,Chao Zhang,Jianping Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ROLoad-PMP，一种轻量级的硬件-软件协同设计方案，用于保护低级软件中的敏感操作不被劫持，通过新增只读加载指令和编译时程序加固机制，在极低的硬件开销(<1.40%)和运行时开销(<0.853%)下提供了比现有方案更广泛且更强的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保护敏感操作（如控制流转移）的方案通常只保护部分目标且性能开销高，难以部署在资源受限的系统（如IoT设备）或低级软件（如内核和裸机固件）上。因此迫切需要一种轻量级、高效的解决方案来保护低级软件中的敏感操作不被攻击者劫持。

Method: 提出ROLoad-PMP硬件-软件协同设计方案：(1)设计新的指令集扩展，仅从具有特定密钥的只读内存区域加载数据，保证指针指向内容的完整性；(2)开发程序加固机制，在编译时分类并将敏感操作的操作数放入不同密钥的只读内存中，运行时使用ROLoad-PMP系列指令加载。基于RISC-V在FPGA上实现了原型系统。

Result: 硬件开销极低（<1.40%），运行时开销可忽略不计（<0.853%），能够支持包括前向边缘控制流完整性在内的多种轻量级防御机制，在安全保证的广度和强度上均优于现有的硬件解决方案（如ARM BTI和Intel CET）。

Conclusion: ROLoad-PMP成功实现了轻量级、高效的保护方案，为资源受限系统和低级软件提供了实用的敏感操作保护机制，在保证安全性的同时保持了极低的性能开销，相比现有方案具有更好的实用性和安全性。

Abstract: A common way for attackers to compromise victim systems is hijacking sensitive operations (e.g., control-flow transfers) with attacker-controlled inputs. Existing solutions in general only protect parts of these targets and have high performance overheads, which are impractical and hard to deploy on systems with limited resources (e.g., IoT devices) or for low-level software like kernels and bare-metal firmware. In this paper, we present a lightweight hardware-software co-design solution ROLoad-PMP to protect sensitive operations from being hijacked for low-level software. First, we propose new instructions, which only load data from read-only memory regions with specific keys, to guarantee the integrity of pointees pointed by (potentially corrupted) data pointers. Then, we provide a program hardening mechanism to protect sensitive operations, by classifying and placing their operands into read-only memory with different keys at compile-time and loading them with ROLoad-PMP-family instructions at runtime. We have implemented an FPGA-based prototype of ROLoad-PMP based on RISC-V, and demonstrated an important defense application, i.e., forward-edge control-flow integrity. Results showed that ROLoad-PMP only costs few extra hardware resources (< 1.40%). Moreover, it enables many lightweight (e.g., with negligible overheads < 0.853%) defenses, and provides broader and stronger security guarantees than existing hardware solutions, e.g., ARM BTI and Intel CET.

</details>


### [103] [YAVIN: A Unified Architecture for Secure Edge Processing in Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13496)
*Shouzhi Fang,William C. Tegge,Md Omar Faruque,Peipei Zhou,Endadul Hoque,Alex K. Jones*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出YAVIN，首个将可信执行环境（TEE）扩展至内存处理（PIM）的统一可信计算基（TCB），在非可信内存总线环境下实现了处理器与内存的安全协同计算，并在后量子密码和边缘大模型场景下显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘计算系统面临多租户安全执行的挑战，而现有的可信执行环境（TEE）仅能在处理器内部建立信任，无法支持在内存中直接进行可信计算。尽管内存处理（PIM）技术能够有效缓解冯·诺伊曼瓶颈，但如何将其与TEE结合以实现跨处理器和内存的安全计算仍是未解难题。

Method: 本文提出YAVIN系统，构建统一可信计算基（TCB），将TEE扩展至处理器和专用内存区域。系统首次在DRAM上实现了LightSaber KEM后量子密码系统和ASCON-128认证加密算法，并针对张量工作负载设计了密码-PIM协同优化方案，通过比特切片排序限制临时明文暴露，在满足认证加密顺序约束的同时最小化性能开销。

Result: 相较于最新的PIM AES实现，YAVIN实现了超过20倍的速度提升。在执行INT8和INT32量化的边缘级大语言模型时，相对于明文执行分别仅产生34%和9.3%的性能开销。

Conclusion: YAVIN成功实现了跨处理器和内存的统一可信计算基，证明了在非可信内存总线环境下进行安全内存处理的可行性，为边缘计算系统中的安全多租户执行提供了新的解决方案，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: Secure, private multi-tenant execution spanning processors, memory, and accelerators remains one of the most significant challenges in modern edge computing systems. Simultaneously, processing-in-memory (PIM) has emerged as an effective approach for reducing the Von Neumann bottleneck by moving computation closer to data. Existing trusted execution environments (TEEs) establish trust only within the processor, protecting data while it traverses untrusted resources such as the memory bus. Consequently, trusted computation cannot be performed directly within memory. We present YAVIN, a unified trusted computing base (TCB) that extends the TEE beyond the processor to encompass both processor execution and a dedicated memory region supporting trusted processing-in-memory execution while treating the memory bus as untrusted. Leveraging the dedicated protected memory regions already established by conventional TEE architectures, YAVIN enables data to be decrypted, processed, and re-encrypted by either processor or PIM execution while remaining within the TEE. To realize this unified TCB, YAVIN presents the first PIM implementations of the LightSaber KEM post-quantum cryptosystem and ASCON-128 authenticated encryption, co-designing both algorithms for efficient DRAM execution to establish and maintain shared cryptographic state. Finally, we demonstrate how cryptography-PIM co-design for tensor-based workloads reorganizes computation to satisfy the ordering constraints imposed by authenticated encryption with minimal performance overhead while simultaneously enabling bit-sliced ordering that limits temporary plaintext exposure. Compared to the latest PIM AES implementation, YAVIN achieves more than a 20x speedup while incurring only 34% and 9.3% overhead when executing INT8 and INT32 quantized edge-class LLMs, respectively, relative to plaintext execution.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Offering Microsecond-Scale Cross-VM Core Elasticity on Colocated Lightweight Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12633)
*Yibo Yan,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了HyperFlux，一种支持弹性并行度调整的超轻量级虚拟机监控器，能在13微秒内跨虚拟机迁移物理核心，在保证高部署密度的同时显著降低尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器平台通过在快速启动的轻量级虚拟机中部署多样化工作负载来提高部署密度。为应对流量突发而过度配置虚拟机会损害密度，而维持高密度又需要基础设施能够以微秒级时间尺度在延迟敏感的突发虚拟机之间迁移物理核心。现有虚拟机方案均无法满足此需求：传统虚拟机通过毫秒级的vCPU热插拔调整核心数，Firecracker在启动时固定核心数，而最快的超轻量级虚拟机则完全放弃了多核执行支持。

Method: 设计并实现HyperFlax，一种基于商用KVM的超轻量级虚拟机监控器，其核心创新是使虚拟机的并行度（即支撑其运行的物理核心数量）在运行时具有弹性。通过高效的核心迁移机制，HyperFlux能够在极短时间内在不同虚拟机之间重新分配物理核心资源，甚至在强制从繁忙的捐赠者回收核心时也能快速完成。

Result: HyperFlux能够在13微秒内跨虚拟机迁移核心（比vCPU热插拔快数个数量级），每个虚拟机仅产生3.2MB内存开销，冷启动时间为1.37毫秒（与最快的超轻量级虚拟机相当），同时独特地支持多核并行。在混合部署场景下，与使用Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享相比，高负载时可将高优先级虚拟机的尾部延迟降低多达10倍；在变化的负载突发下，相比使用cgroup和vCPU热插拔，能够提供更低且更稳定的尾部延迟。

Conclusion: HyperFlux成功弥合了快速启动、低内存开销与多核执行支持之间的差距，证明了在无服务器场景下实现弹性资源分配的可行性。通过微秒级的核心迁移能力，HyperFlux在保持高部署密度的同时有效保护了尾部延迟，为无服务器基础设施的资源管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly colocate many diverse workloads, each in a fast-booting, memory-lean virtual machine (VM), to improve deployment density. Overprovisioning each VM for its peak protects tail latency during traffic bursts but hurts density; maintaining high density while effectively protecting tail latency requires the infrastructure to be able to shift physical cores, at a microsecond timescale, to whichever latency-sensitive VM is bursting and reclaim them as the burst subsides. No VM substrate delivers this: conventional VMs resize a guest's cores only through a millisecond-scale vCPU hot-plug path, Firecracker fixes a VM's core count at boot, and the ultralight VMs that boot fastest drop multicore execution entirely.
  We present HyperFlux, a commodity-KVM ultralight VM substrate that makes a VM's parallelism width (the number of physical cores backing it) elastic at runtime. We show that HyperFlux can move a core across VMs in merely 13$μ$s, even when forcibly reclaiming it from a busy donor, orders of magnitude faster than vCPU hot-plug. A HyperFlux VM incurs only a 3.2MB memory footprint and can cold-boot in 1.37ms, on par with the fastest-booting ultralight VMs, while uniquely supporting multicore parallelism. Under colocation, it can reduce high-priority VMs' tail latency by up to 10x under high load compared to static core-sharing with Firecracker and Cloud Hypervisor, and deliver a lower and more stable tail latency compared to using cgroup and vCPU hot-plug under changing load bursts.

</details>


### [105] [InFactPlanner: Planning Sustainable Geo-Distributed LLM Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12915)
*Nicoletta Tsiopani,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了InFactPlanner，一个用于LLM推理可持续数据中心部署的假设分析决策支持框架，能够在无需实际部署的情况下评估和比较不同部署方案的环境影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理需求的快速增长，可持续性关注点从一次性训练转向持续服务。运营商需要在建设大规模基础设施前比较不同部署方案，但直接测量成本高、耗时长且往往不可行。

Method: InFactPlanner是一个基于轨迹驱动的框架，结合查询轨迹、硬件模型配置、候选站点配置、PUE/WUE参数、可再生能源生成模型和时变电网碳强度，估算功耗、能耗、碳排放、用水量、延迟和服务器利用率。该框架将底层服务效果抽象为可配置的硬件模型配置，支持快速比较站点选择、容量配置、硬件、模型、可再生能源集成和路由选择。

Result: 能耗核算管线验证误差小于10%，框架在多数据中心和服务器规模下具有良好的可扩展性。决策分析显示：可持续性最优选择与延迟最优选择可能不同；部署的碳价值高度依赖于当地电网结构。

Conclusion: InFactPlanner为LLM推理部署提供了有效的假设分析工具，揭示了可持续性优化与性能优化之间的权衡关系，强调了电网碳强度对部署决策的重要影响。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLM inference is shifting sustainability concerns from one-time training to continuous serving, where infrastructure decisions shape energy use, carbon emissions, water consumption, and service quality. Yet operators often need to compare deployment alternatives before large-scale infrastructure is built, making direct measurement costly, slow, and sometimes infeasible. We present InFactPlanner, a trace-driven decision-support framework for what-if analysis of sustainable AI data center deployment for LLM inference across single and geo-distributed sites. InFactPlanner combines query traces, hardware-model profiles, candidate site configurations, PUE/WUE parameters, renewable generation models, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate power, energy, carbon emissions, water use, latency, and server utilization. The framework abstracts low-level serving effects into configurable hardware-model profiles, enabling rapid comparison of site selection, capacity placement, hardware, model, renewable integration, and routing choices. We validate the energy accounting pipeline by reproducing reference LLM inference energy estimates with less than 10% deviation, evaluate scalability across multiple data centers and server counts, and demonstrate scenario-driven decision analyses for hardware selection, renewable placement, geographic deployment, and carbon-aware routing. Our results show that sustainability-optimal choices can differ from latency-optimal ones, and that the carbon value of deployment depends strongly on the local grid mix.

</details>


### [106] [Efficient Randomized LL/SC that Preserves History Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12946)
*Dante Bencivenga,Homa Habashi,Philipp Woelfel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个随机化算法，使用O(nτ+m)空间复杂度实现m个线性化LL/SC对象，达到已知下界；且满足QHI特性，使STOC 2025的哈希算法可在实际硬件上高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LL/SC是关键同步原语但硬件不直接支持。现有确定性算法空间复杂度为Ω(n²τ+m)，远高于理论下界。STOC 2025提出需要Θ(m)个硬件LL/SC对象的QHI动态哈希算法，但缺乏高效且满足QHI特性的软件实现。

Method: 设计随机化算法，结合CAS、FADD和寄存器三种基础对象，针对弱自适应对手进行分析。算法满足静止历史无关（QHI）特性：无待处理操作时，内部状态仅由m个LL/SC对象的值唯一确定。

Result: 在n进程、每个进程最多τ个未完成LL操作的条件下，用O(nτ+m)个基础对象实现m个线性化LL/SC对象。当m=O(1)时达到已知下界。假设m=Ω(n)时，可在不增加步数和空间复杂度的情况下支持STOC 2025的哈希算法。

Conclusion: 证明了随机化可突破确定性算法的空间下界限制，首次展示了如何在现有硬件上高效实现QHI LL/SC对象，为将理论算法（如STOC 2025哈希）落地实际系统提供了关键支撑。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of implementing $m$ linearizable LL/SC objects with constant expected step complexity in a system of $n$ processes, using bounded base objects commonly available in hardware. Assuming that each process may have at most $τ$ outstanding LL operations, the best known deterministic algorithm requires $Ω(n^2τ+ m)$ base objects (CAS and registers) [Blelloch and Wei, DISC 2020]. Previously, no comparable randomized algorithm was known.
  By employing randomization and FADD in addition to CAS and registers, we obtain a space bound of $O(nτ+m)$ against the weak adaptive adversary. For $m=O(1)$ this matches a lower bound for algorithms using CAS and registers [Aghazadeh and Woelfel, PODC 2015].
  In addition, our object can be employed by quiescently history-independent (QHI) algorithms: Whenever no operation on the object is pending and no process has an outstanding LL operation, its internal memory state is uniquely determined by the values of the $m$ LL/SC objects. An important application is a QHI dynamic hashing algorithm presented at STOC 2025, which uses $Θ(m)$ hardware LL/SC objects to maintain a hash table of size $m$ [Attiya, Bender, Farach-Colton, Oshman, and Schiller, STOC 2025]. But LL/SC is not available in hardware, and prior to our work no wait-free or efficiently lock-free software implementation of LL/SC with similar properties was known. Our work demonstrates that one can actually implement the hashing algorithm on available hardware, without an asymptotic increase in step and space complexity, under the reasonable assumption that $m=Ω(n)$.

</details>


### [107] [TEMPO: Makespan-Aware Expert-Parallel Load Balancing Across Memory- and Compute-Bound Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13057)
*Jie Li,Chenxin Jia,Jinliang Shen,Cunzhuang Liu,Ruiyi Ding,Jianwen Xian,Kang He,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了专家并行MoE服务中专家计算时间的非线性特性（低负载下HBM权重流占主导，高负载下分组GEMM填充开销），提出了基于makespan的调度器\sys{}，在混合负载场景下最高提升15.5%吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EP MoE调度器（EPLB、LPLB、UltraEP、METRO）假设专家计算时间与token数或激活专家数呈线性关系，但实测发现该假设不成立：低于阈值时HBM权重流主导（开销与激活副本相关），高于阈值时分组GEMM存在128-tile填充开销。不同负载下最优调度策略会翻转，需要新的调度模型。

Method: 提出max-affine性能模型$t=\max(a+bG, c+\beta N)$刻画两种负载模式；将每批次调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题（两GPU全复制场景NP难，退化情况可解）；设计\sys{}调度器在毫秒级求解，与SGLang集成，将调度与计数收集融合为单内核操作。

Result: 在8-GPU Testbed A上，\sys{}在所有场景保持与最优固定基线差距1%以内，在负载混合场景获胜达15.5%。端到端测试中，Qwen3-235B提升4-6%吞吐量，p99延迟降低15.6%；DeepSeek-V3（通信主导）仅显示机制开销。相位图可预测部署结果。

Conclusion: MoE专家计算时间具有双相特性，不存在通用最优调度策略。\sys{}通过makespan感知调度在混合负载场景取得显著收益，相位图可预测不同场景下的调度效果，为MoE部署提供决策依据。

Abstract: In expert-parallel (EP) MoE serving, every layer synchronizes at the slowest GPU. Dispatchers balance token counts (EPLB, LPLB, UltraEP) or activated-expert counts (METRO), assuming expert time is linear in one. Measurements on two datacenter GPU generations show it is neither: below $\nstar\!\approx\!156$--$168$ tokens, HBM weight streaming dominates---cost attaches to \emph{activated replicas}, not tokens; above it, grouped GEMM rounds tokens to 128-tile $M$-tiles, so \emph{splitting} an expert adds padded compute. A max-affine profile $t=\max(a+bG,\,c+βN)$ captures both regimes. Realistic decode batches hold hot experts in the linear regime and cold in the flat \emph{simultaneously}; recorded batches show proxy dispatches differ by $1.4$--$1.6\times$ in modeled block time (p95 up to $1.7\times$), and \emph{which} proxy wins flips with the regime. We formalize per-batch dispatch as a fixed-charge makespan problem---NP-hard on two fully replicated GPUs, polynomial in degenerate limits---and present \sys{}, a makespan-aware dispatcher solving it in milliseconds off the critical path; its SGLang integration runs out-of-process and fuses dispatch with count collection into one in-graph kernel. Anchored by an 8-GPU Testbed~A microbenchmark, \sys{} stays within 1\% of the best fixed baseline everywhere and wins by up to $15.5\%$ where regimes mix. End-to-end on Testbed~B, Qwen3-235B (inside the win region) gains $4$--$6\%$ throughput and cuts p99 latency by ${\sim}15.6\%$; DeepSeek-V3 (outside, communication-dominated) shows only mechanism cost. A phase diagram, not a universal win, is the claim: it predicts both outcomes before deployment.

</details>


### [108] [Meshlib: In-Process Policy Enforcement for Sidecar-less Service Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13107)
*Habib Mostafaei,Tom van Liempd*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Meshlib，一种基于Cilium的无Sidecar服务网格扩展方案，通过将L7策略执行分离到应用进程内库，实现了最低的端到端延迟，同时保持了服务网格的安全语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务网格依赖per-pod的sidecar代理，导致每个请求都需要冗余的应用层解析，引入显著的延迟和资源开销。如何在消除sidecar的同时不妥协安全保证，是当前面临的核心挑战。

Method: 提出Meshlib，作为Cilium控制平面扩展构建的无sidecar服务网格方案：将L7策略执行下沉到应用进程内的非侵入式应用绑定库，同时将传输层身份识别和路由委托给Cilium的eBPF数据平面。

Result: 在TrainTicket基准测试中（37个服务、126条安全策略），与Istio、Linkerd和未修改的Cilium相比，Meshlib在所有评估配置中实现了最低的端到端延迟，资源开销相当。

Conclusion: Meshlib成功消除了sidecar引入的延迟，保持了服务网格的安全语义，并与未修改服务完全互操作，支持现有部署的增量采用。

Abstract: Service meshes facilitate service-to-service communication and enforce security policies in microservice architectures. However, they often depend on per-pod sidecar proxies, which introduce significant latency and resource overhead due to redundant application-layer parsing on every request. Eliminating sidecars without compromising security guarantees remains a central challenge. To address this, we introduce Meshlib, a sidecar-less service mesh extension built on Cilium as a control-plane extension. Meshlib incorporates a non-intrusive application-bound library that enforces Layer-7 policies within the application process, while delegating transport-level identity and routing to Cilium's eBPF-based data plane. This separation of responsibilities removes sidecar-induced latency and maintains the security semantics of the service mesh. The architecture remains fully interoperable with unmodified services, enabling incremental adoption within existing deployments. We evaluate Meshlib against Istio, Linkerd, and unmodified Cilium on the TrainTicket benchmark, enforcing 126 security policies across 37 services and show that it achieves the lowest end-to-end latency of all evaluated configurations with comparable resource overhead.

</details>


### [109] [Validation-Centric AI-Assisted GPU Porting of a 250,000+ Line Legacy Weather Simulation Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13122)
*Tetsuya Hoshino,Masaya Kato,Kazuhisa Tsuboki,Daichi Mukunoki,Takahiro Katagiri,Toshihiro Hanawa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流程，并通过CReSS气象模拟代码的案例研究验证了其有效性，成功移植162个内核并实现5.1倍加速，同时检测到5个内核的数值偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型科学应用程序不仅是老旧代码库，更是经过长期开发和验证的科学资产。在GPU移植过程中，必须在适应GPU为中心的HPC系统的同时保持其科学有效性，这需要验证驱动的移植方法。

Method: 采用AI代理从超过25万行Fortran气象模拟代码CReSS中提取OpenMP区域，生成基于转储的内核基准测试，应用OpenACC转换，并通过与转储参考数据的逐元素比较和应用级验证来验证结果。使用真实台风模拟作为测试用例。

Result: 成功为162个目标内核生成数值验证的GPU实现，在实用的开发成本内实现了5.1倍应用级加速。检测到5个内核中的数值偏差，原因包括浮点数和内在函数差异、阈值敏感的分支分歧和抵消效应。

Conclusion: 对于需要基于转储验证的大型遗留科学应用，实际的AI辅助GPU移植必须管理会话跨度的上下文、运行时状态重建，以及从小型静态分析遗漏中恢复的成本。AI辅助GPU移植不仅需要代码生成，更需要以验证为中心的工作流程设计。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made CLI-based AI agents a practical tool for accelerating GPU porting of large legacy scientific applications. Such applications, however, are not merely old code bases; they are scientific assets whose credibility has been accumulated through long-term development, comparison with observations, and use in domain studies. GPU porting must therefore preserve this scientific validity while adapting the implementation to GPU-centric HPC systems. This paper presents a validation-centric AI-assisted GPU porting workflow through a case study of CReSS, a legacy Fortran weather simulation code with more than 250,000 lines. The workflow uses an AI agent to extract OpenMP regions, generate dump-based kernel benchmarks from physically meaningful simulation states, apply OpenACC transformations, and validate results through element-wise comparison with dumped reference data and application-level validation. Using a real typhoon simulation, the workflow produced numerically validated GPU implementations for 162 target kernels and achieved a 5.1x application-level speedup within practical wall-clock development cost. In particular, it detected numerical discrepancies in five kernels caused by floating-point and intrinsic-function differences, including threshold-sensitive branch divergence and cancellation effects, enabling feedback to the application developers. The case study suggests that, for large legacy scientific applications requiring dump-based validation, practical AI-assisted GPU porting must manage session-spanning context, runtime-state reconstruction, and costly recovery from small static-analysis omissions. These findings demonstrate that AI-assisted GPU porting requires not only code generation, but validation-centric workflow design.

</details>


### [110] [LipCache: A Local Inference Proxy with Certified Caching for Edge Image Classification Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13144)
*Zhengzhe Xiang,Yinlin Chen,Fuli Ying,Binbin Zhou,Hailiang Zhao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了LipCache，一种针对图像分类的认证语义缓存框架，通过引入受Lipschitz约束的GuardNet网络，将缓存命中判定从经验阈值测试转变为具有理论边界的几何认证决策，在提升推理速度的同时保证了缓存重用的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义缓存方法主要依赖经验相似性阈值，虽然能提高命中率，但在决策边界附近容易引入静默误分类问题，无法保证缓存重用的可靠性。随着边缘端视觉服务向低延迟、高吞吐量场景扩展，亟需一种既能降低视觉模型推理成本又不牺牲可靠性的解决方案。

Method: LipCache框架在保留已部署主模型MainNet不变的前提下，引入了一个轻量级网络GuardNet。GuardNet将输入映射到受Lipschitz约束的低维特征空间，并根据局部分类边界和分类头的谱范数计算每个样本的认证重用半径。运行时，只有当查询特征落在认证重用球内时才重用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet进行推理，从而将缓存命中判定转化为具有明确理论边界的几何认证决策。

Result: 在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN等标准图像分类任务上，LipCache实现了最高1.65倍的实际加速，端到端精度损失有限，且所有接受的缓存命中均满足GuardNet侧的认证一致性条件。在Tiny-ImageNet多类别扩展中，增强的GuardNet训练方案在保持100%认证一致性率的同时显著提高了缓存命中率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于每样本认证重用的方法能够有效减少主模型回退并保持理论一致性，为边缘端可靠缓存辅助推理提供了一种可行方案，证明了在保证可靠性的前提下实现推理加速的可能性。

Abstract: As edge-side vision services continue to expand toward low-latency, high-throughput scenarios, reducing the inference cost of vision models without sacrificing reliability has become a central concern. Existing semantic caching methods largely rely on empirical similarity thresholds; while such thresholds improve hit rates, they tend to introduce silent misclassifications near decision boundaries. To address this, we propose \texttt{LipCache}, a certified semantic caching framework for image classification. Without modifying the existing deployed main model, \texttt{MainNet}, the framework introduces a lightweight network, \texttt{GuardNet}, that maps inputs into a low-dimensional feature space subject to a Lipschitz constraint. It then computes a per-sample certified reuse radius from the local classification margin and the spectral norm of the classification head. At runtime, a cached result is reused only when the query feature falls inside the certified reuse ball; otherwise, the query falls back to \texttt{MainNet}. Thus, cache hits are transformed from empirical threshold tests into geometric certification decisions with explicit theoretical boundaries. Across standard image classification tasks like CIFAR, Tiny-ImageNet, and SVHN, \texttt{LipCache} achieves a measured speedup of up to $1.65\times$ with limited end-to-end accuracy degradation, while all accepted cache hits satisfy the \texttt{GuardNet}-side certified-consistency condition. Furthermore, an enhanced \texttt{GuardNet} training recipe substantially improves cache hit rates in the Tiny-ImageNet multi-class extension while maintaining a certified-consistency rate of $100\%$. These results demonstrate that per-sample certified reuse can reduce main-model fallback while preserving theoretical consistency, providing a feasible approach to reliable cache-assisted inference at the edge.

</details>


### [111] [Triangle-Free Coloring in LOCAL via Resilient Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13357)
*Peter Davies-Peck,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了分布式三角形图着色算法，通过引入"韧性"定义，将LLL实例求解复杂度降至$\log^{O(1)}\log n$，突破了LLL瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lovász局部引理(LLL)在分布式算法中具有重要地位，但其求解复杂度高，成为三角形图着色等应用的瓶颈。现有算法需多次调用分布式LLL，而LLL本身需要$O(\log_Δn)$或$O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮次。

Method: 基于Pettie-Su算法进行改进，引入Davies[SODA 2023]提出的"韧性"(resilience)定义，使得生成的LLL实例可在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内求解，避免LLL成为算法瓶颈。

Result: 获得了$O(k)+\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮次的三角形图着色算法（使用$\fracΔ{k}$颜色，$k$可达$(\frac14-\varepsilon)\lnΔ$）；首次实现$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮次的$o(Δ)$颜色三角形图着色；同时给出girth-5图着色的伴随算法，使用$(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\lnΔ$颜色。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功将LLL步骤从算法瓶颈中解放，显著提升了三角形图着色等问题的分布式算法效率，复杂度达到最优的$\log^{O(1)}\log n$级别。

Abstract: The Lovász Local Lemma (LLL) is a probabilistic tool that has been shown to be of central importance in the study of distributed algorithms. For example, the constructive LLL is known to be complete for the class of locally-checkable labeling problems with $o(\log n)$ randomized complexities in the LOCAL model. One classic application of the LLL is in coloring graphs with some sparse structure, such as triangle-free graphs. Triangle-free coloring therefore serves as a benchmark problem for techniques for sublogarithmic randomized distributed algorithms. The state-of-the-art distributed triangle-free coloring algorithm of Pettie and Su [ICALP 2013, Information and Computation 2015] uses $\fracΔ{k}$ colors (where $k$ can be up to $(\frac14 - \varepsilon)\ln Δ$) and consists of $O(k+\log^* n)$ applications of the distributed LLL. However, the distributed LLL is itself a difficult problem; despite significant study, the fastest algorithms known require $O(\log_Δn)$ or $O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds. In this work, we adapt the Pettie-Su's algorithm so that the resulting LLL instances can be solved in $\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, by employing the 'resilience' definition of Davies [SODA 2023]. This gives an $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ complexity (since the LLL is not needed when $k= \log^{ω(1)}\log n$), essentially causing the LLL steps to no longer be the bottleneck of the algorithm. As a corollary we obtain the first $\log^{O(1)}\log n$-round algorithms for coloring triangle-free graphs with $o(Δ)$ colors. The same framework also yields a companion girth-$5$ algorithm, using $(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\ln Δ$ colors in $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, matching the best known existential upper bound for the number of colors.

</details>


### [112] [Fast Tendermint: Speeding Up a Foundational Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13434)
*Preston Vander Vos,Daniel Cason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Fast Tendermint，一种在n > 5f条件下实现两步通信的BFT共识协议改进方案，同时保持了原始Tendermint的领导者轮换机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Tendermint协议需要三个通信步骤才能达成共识，而区块链系统对低延迟的需求日益增长。最近研究表明在f < n/5条件下可以用两步通信解决共识问题，这启发作者优化Tendermint的性能。

Method: 将Tendermint的prevote和precommit两个步骤合并为单个投票步骤，并融合locked和valid状态。协议在正常情况下只需两步通信即可达成决定，同时保持Tendermint原有的领导者轮换结构。

Result: 成功设计出Fast Tendermint协议，在n > 5f的设定下实现了两步通信的共识决策。提供了协议的一致性、有效性和终止性的形式化证明，并使用Quint语言（TLA+的现代语法）给出了形式化规范，用于模型检测。

Conclusion: Fast Tendermint成功地将Tendermint适配到更严格的拜占庭容错设定（n > 5f），在保持原有架构优势的同时显著降低了通信延迟，为区块链系统提供了更高效的共识方案。

Abstract: Tendermint is among the most widely studied and deployed Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, owing in part to its native leader-rotation mechanism that subsumes complex view changes. Like most partially-synchronous BFT protocols, Tendermint tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes and decides in three communication steps. Motivated by the push for lower-latency blockchains, a recent line of work shows that consensus can be solved in two communication steps when $f < n/5$. We present Fast Tendermint, an adaptation of Tendermint to the $n > 5f$ setting that decides in two communication steps in the good case, while preserving Tendermint's leader-rotation structure. Fast Tendermint collapses Tendermint's prevote and precommit steps into a single voting step and merges the $locked$ and $valid$ state. We give proofs of agreement, validity, and termination, and a formal specification in Quint, a modern surface syntax for TLA+, used to model-check the protocol.

</details>


### [113] [OpScale: Operator-level Provisioning and Autoscaling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13499)
*Xingqi Cui,Chieh-Jan Mike Liang,Ziang Tang,Jiarong Xing,Haoran Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了OpScale，一种面向LLM推理服务的算子级（operator-level）自动扩缩容框架，通过利用算子异构性实现细粒度资源管理，在满足SLO的同时显著降低GPU资源消耗和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务集群的自动扩缩容方案将整个模型作为单一扩缩容单元，无法捕捉推理负载的细粒度动态特征，导致突发负载下SLO违约或GPU资源利用率低下。研究表明存在显著的算子异构性，为算子级弹性扩缩容提供了可能性。

Method: 提出了OpScale算子级编排框架，包含四个核心组件：性能分析（profiling）、资源供给（provisioning）、算子放置（placement）和运行时服务（runtime serving），以解决算子级细粒度管理带来的高复杂度和状态空间爆炸问题。

Result: 在生产负载轨迹上评估（最多40块A100和24块GB200），OpScale在满足SLO约束下减少36.3%的GPU用量和28%的功耗，或在固定成本预算下实现44%的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 算子级细粒度扩缩容能够有效捕捉LLM推理负载的动态特征，相比传统粗粒度方案在资源效率、能耗和吞吐量方面均有显著优势，为云GPU集群的LLM服务提供了新的资源管理范式。

Abstract: Achieving cost efficiency while meeting strict user-facing SLOs (e.g., time-to-first-token) remains a fundamental challenge for cloud GPU clusters serving large language models (LLMs). Autoscaling is the key mechanism for cluster resource management, yet a basic system design question is open for serving LLMs: what should be the unit of scaling? Existing approaches primarily treat the entire model as a monolithic scaling unit--simple but unable to capture the fine-grained dynamics of inference workloads. As a result, such coarse-grained scaling often leads to either SLO violations under bursty demand or significant GPU under-utilization.
  Our characterization reveals substantial operator heterogeneity, exposing operator-level elasticity as a viable scaling primitive. We present OpScale, a practical operator-level orchestration framework of profiling, provisioning, placement, and runtime serving. OpScale is designed to tackle the high complexity and the space explosion problem, arising from operating at this finer granularity. Evaluated with production traces on up to 40 A100s and 24 GB200s, OpScale attains SLOs with up to 36.3% fewer GPUs and 28% less power, or achieves 44% higher throughput under fixed cost budgets.

</details>


### [114] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了RoutePack，一种层次化规划方法，通过协调专家重路由和数据打包来优化MoE强化学习训练，在两个模型上分别实现了8.85%和14.89%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE强化学习训练面临两个相互耦合的负载均衡问题：序列组成决定数据并行微批中的密集注意力计算，token路由决定专家并行等级上的稀疏专家计算。单独优化任一方面都可能使瓶颈转移至另一方面。

Method: RoutePack采用层次化规划：首先基于聚合路由需求在各MoE层独立放置专家，然后将样本打包到最小的可行token限制执行行中，使用投影的EDP分片感知目标优化数据并行布局。该方法结合了窗口归一化的线性-二次注意力代理和每层物理EP秩峰值，最小化最慢EDP分片的累积成本。并行群体退火算法在保持样本覆盖、容量、非空单元、相等微批数和通信拓扑的同时搜索可行布局。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash模型上，专家重路由分别提升平均token吞吐量3.80%和10.50%，路由感知打包额外提升4.86%和3.98%。总体吞吐量分别提升8.85%和14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过协调专家重路由和数据打包有效解决了MoE RL训练中的负载均衡问题，在保持状态一致性和兼容现有MoE内核的前提下显著提升了训练吞吐量，证明了联合优化注意力和专家负载的重要性。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LoKiFormer，一种新型LLM架构，通过引入局部融合注意力(LFA)和知识记忆模块(KMM)解决了自注意力缺乏局部性偏置和MoE耦合存储与计算的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练时存在两个主要效率瓶颈：(1) 自注意力机制缺乏显式的局部性归纳偏置，导致对序列内部局部信息的冗余建模；(2) 混合专家模型(MoE)隐式地将知识存储与计算路径耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 提出LoKiFormer架构，在标准解码器上增加两个专用模块：(1) 局部融合注意力(LFA)，将卷积融合到注意力中，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力在更具信息量的表示上运行；(2) 知识记忆模块(KMM)，引入参数化键值记忆，在可寻址槽中显式存储全局知识，解耦存储与计算并支持直接知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练阶段的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了其在现有LLM架构中的优越性。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过LFA和KMM两个模块实现了局部信息和全局知识的高效整合，显著提升了预训练效率，为LLM架构设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [116] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用免费卫星数据开发预测模型，识别喜马拉雅地区冰湖溃决、滑坡等灾害的易感性和触发时机，发现天气数据能有效预测触发时机（ROC 0.73-0.83），但地形特征预测易感性的能力有限（0.54-0.76），简单梯度提升模型在多数情况下优于深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前可行性研究表明卫星雷达干涉测量能识别正在失稳的冰湖，气象数据能标记湖泊处于风险期的时段，但缺乏实际的预测模型。为填补这一空白，研究旨在开发和评估能够预测哪些地点易感以及何时触发灾害的模型。

Method: 研究基于HMAGLOFDB数据库的589个有日期记录的溃决事件和数千个编录滑坡事件，采用空间交叉验证方法（按完整地图瓦片划分），将每个事件与相似但未发生失稳的地点进行匹配对比。研究测试了三种相关灾害：大型冰碛坝和冰坝溃决、降雨触发滑坡、以及冰川周边小规模洪水。针对每种灾害分别回答"哪个地点易感"和"何时触发"两个独立问题，使用前期天气数据预测触发时机，使用地形特征评估易感性，对比了五种深度学习模型与简单梯度提升基线模型。

Result: 天气数据预测触发时机的ROC值达到：大型溃决0.73、滑坡0.83、小规模洪水0.82。地形特征预测易感性的实际ROC值为：大型溃决0.76、滑坡0.71、小规模洪水0.54（仅相当于随机猜测）。模型在尼泊尔地区单独表现最佳，ROC达到0.89。五种深度学习模型未能显著超越简单梯度提升基线模型，三种在滑坡预测上略有提升但不显著。对于冰湖灾害，基线模型表现最佳，可通过基于地形粗糙度和季风降雨的三规则决策树复现结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明免费卫星数据在预测灾害触发时机方面表现良好，但在预测易感性方面能力有限，特别是对于小规模洪水事件。深度学习模型相比简单基线模型没有显著优势。研究最终提供了尼泊尔地区的优先监测名单，作为风险优先级的辅助工具而非预测结果，并指出免费数据的应用局限性。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [117] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH架构，通过引入内容路由的状态缓存机制，在保持计算效率的同时有效扩展了状态空间模型的内存容量，显著提升了长序列任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构虽然具有强大的长上下文检索能力，但其二次方计算复杂度和线性增长的KV缓存限制了效率；而循环神经网络替代方案虽然解码高效，但由于早期关联会被后续更新覆盖，在需要召回的任务上表现不佳。作者旨在解决这一内存容量与计算效率之间的根本矛盾。

Method: MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History）架构：(1)周期性地将累积的循环状态检查点缓存为状态锚点；(2)将每个锚点与紧凑的内容条件锚点键关联；(3)维护一个随上下文长度增长的内存库，提供历史分辨率与内存成本间的可控权衡；(4)在每个token生成锚点查询，以注意力方式聚合所有历史可用状态锚点。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上持续优于多个线性注意力变体，证明了内容路由状态缓存能有效增强循环长程记忆能力。

Conclusion: MARCH成功实现了计算效率与长序列建模能力的平衡，通过可扩展的内存库机制突破了固定维度状态空间的限制，为构建高效的序列模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [118] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VGG-MADiffRL算法和MDCA架构解决多AUV协同目标跟踪问题，实现更快收敛、更高精度和更稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多AUV自组网目标跟踪面临水声通信受限、动态拓扑和海洋扰动等挑战。现有多智能体强化学习方法存在高维联合状态-动作建模、噪声敏感策略生成等问题，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能下降。

Method: 1) 建立声纳检测机制和洋流扰动模型，将多AUV协同跟踪建模为MDP；2) 设计三层级MDCA架构（全局智能控制层、局部在线训练层、物理执行层）；3) 提出VGG-MADiffRL算法，基于扩散策略引入价值梯度引导反向去噪过程，采用双价值网络联合优化和软目标更新缓解过估计问题。

Result: 实验表明VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中持续实现更快收敛速度、更高跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了多AUV协同跟踪中的训练不稳定和性能退化问题，具有重要的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [119] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的生成模型理论框架，将多种生成模型表述为同一主作用的路径积分形式，并基于MSRJD形式开发了一种单环修正方法，可将确定性采样器的误差从53%显著降低至1.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型）缺乏统一的理论框架，作者希望建立一个统一的表述来揭示不同生成模型之间的内在联系，并基于场论方法发展改进的采样方法。

Method: 将生成建模表述为路径积分问题，引入Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de Dominicis (MSRJD)形式分离自由概率流与相互作用概率流，利用图解摄动理论进行展开，获得确定性采样器的单环修正。

Result: 在可解漂移和非线性漂移上验证了方法的有效性，单环修正将树级误差从53%降低至1.6%；不完美学习到的分数作为插入项进入理论框架，产生响应加权的分数匹配目标函数。

Conclusion: 该框架成功统一了多种生成模型，展示了场论方法在生成建模中的应用价值，为确定性采样器的改进提供了理论工具，并为对称等变漂移设计提供了有效的算符展开方法。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [120] [Multi-perspective Imbalance-Conscious 6G Beamforming Optimization and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12929)
*Chukwunonso Henry Nwokoye,Blessing Oluchi Iloka,Chikwue V. Umeugoji,Christopher Anene Egemba,Nnenna D. Duroha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了6G-IoT波束成形优化问题，发现网络特征预测能力最优，部署环境和设备类型主导聚类，带宽、IoT传感器和移动性为关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G网络和物联网技术的快速发展，波束成形优化成为提升网络性能的关键技术。然而，现有研究缺乏对多类型特征预测能力的系统比较，且对网络场景聚类模式的理解不足。因此，需要开展系统的机器学习研究，以识别最优特征组和提升6GBO性能的有效方法。

Method: 采用监督学习和无监督学习相结合的方法。监督学习方面，比较网络特征、环境特征、设备特征和视觉特征四组特征的预测能力，进行不平衡感知实验。无监督学习方面，使用K-means、DBSCAN和层次聚类方法对网络场景进行聚类分析。通过Elbow、轮廓系数和Davies-Bouldin指数评估聚类效果，并采用可解释性分析确定特征重要性。

Result: 实验结果表明：网络特征组的预测能力优于设备、环境和视觉特征组，在召回率、F1分数和ROC-AUC值上均有体现。聚类分析显示部署环境和设备类型（而非移动性属性）主要影响聚类效果。可解释性分析揭示带宽、IoT传感器和移动性在各特征组中具有更高的全局特征重要性。

Conclusion: 网络特征是6G-IoT波束成形优化的最优预测因子，部署环境和设备类型是网络场景聚类的主导因素。未来研究将应用深度学习和强化学习技术预测吞吐量/延迟，或优化由SNR增强等性能指标确定的奖励函数，以进一步提升6GBO的智能化水平。

Abstract: The study presents a systematic machine learning (ML) study of 6G-IoT beamforming optimization (6GBO) using supervised and unsupervised approaches. We compared the predictive power of network, environmental, device, and vision feature groups for 6GBO. Additionally, it addressed other unsupervised perspectives that can enhance 6GBO, including clustering network scenarios using methods such as K-means, DBSCAN, and hierarchical clustering. Several imbalance-aware experiments revealed that network features possess better prediction power than device, environmental, and vision feature groups, as evidenced by their recall, F1-score and ROC-AUC values. For unsupervised ML exploration (assessed using Elbow, Silhouette score, and Davies-Bouldin Index methods), the results indicate that the deployment environment and type of device primarily influence clustering, rather than mobility-based attributes. Furthermore, the explainability analysis showed that bandwidth, IoT sensors, and mobility possess higher global feature importance across the feature groups. In the future, we would apply deep and reinforcement learning techniques to predict throughput/latency or to optimize rewards determined by performance indicators like SNR enhancement

</details>


### [121] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
